\u043d\u0443\u0436\u043d\u043e\"\"\"\n        current_time = time.time()\n        \n        if len(self.samples_buffer) < self.min_frames_for_check:\n            return\n        \n        try:\n            # \u041e\u0431\u044a\u0435\u0434\u0438\u043d\u044f\u0435\u043c \u0432\u0441\u0435 \u0441\u044d\u043c\u043f\u043b\u044b \u0432 \u043e\u0434\u0438\u043d \u043c\u0430\u0441\u0441\u0438\u0432\n            all_samples = np.concatenate(self.samples_buffer, axis=1)\n            \n            # \u0412\u044b\u0447\u0438\u0441\u043b\u044f\u0435\u043c \u043c\u0435\u0442\u0440\u0438\u043a\u0438\n            max_amplitude = np.max(np.abs(all_samples))\n            rms = np.sqrt(np.mean(all_samples ** 2))\n            \n            warning_type = None\n            \n            # \u041f\u0440\u043e\u0432\u0435\u0440\u043a\u0430 \u043d\u0430 \u043a\u043b\u0438\u043f\u043f\u0438\u043d\u0433 (\u0441\u043b\u0438\u0448\u043a\u043e\u043c \u0433\u0440\u043e\u043c\u043a\u043e)\n            if max_amplitude > self.loud_threshold:\n                warning_type = \"mic_too_loud\"\n            \n            # \u041f\u0440\u043e\u0432\u0435\u0440\u043a\u0430 \u043d\u0430        \n        except Exception as e:\n            logger.error(f\"Error in audio monitoring: {e}\")\n\n    async def _send_warning(self, warning_type: str, current_time: float) -> None:\n        \"\"\"\u041e\u0442\u043f\u0440\u0430\u0432\u0438\u0442\u044c \u043f\u0440\u0435\u0434\u0443\u043f\u0440\u0435\u0436\u0434\u0435\u043d\u0438\u0435 \u0447\u0435\u0440\u0435\u0437 callback \u0441 self.warning_callback(warning_type)\n                logger.info(f\"Audio warning sent: {warning_type}\")\n            except Exception as e:\n                logger.error(f\"Error calling warning callback: {e}\")\n"}
{"path": "./src_core/processors/recorder_node.py", "content": "import asyncio\nimport wave\nfrom typing import Optional\n\nimport numpy as np\nfrom av import AudioFrame\n\nfrom ..utils.config import RECORDINGS_DIR\nimport logging\n\nlogger = logging.getLogger(\"audio.RecorderNode\")\n\n\nfrom .base import ConsumerNode\n\n\nclass RecorderNode(ConsumerNode):\n    \"\"\"\n    Recorder node consuming normalized frames from a source node (subscribe()) and\n    periodically flushing to WAV files with fixed batch size.\n\n    Assumes frames are s16 planar with consistent sample_rate, channels and\n    fixed samples per frame.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, source_node, *, batch_frames: int = 128) -> None:\n        super().__init__(source_node)\n        self.batch_frames = int(batch_frames)\n        self._frames: list[np.ndarray] = []\n        self._idx: int = 0\n        self._channels: Optional[int] = None\n        self._samplerate: Optional[int] = None\n        self._task: Optional[asyncio.Task] = None\n        self._stopped: bool = False\n\n    async def start(self) -> None:\n        await super().start()\n\n    async def stop(self) -> None:\n        if self._stopped:\n            return\n        self._stopped = True\n        if self._task is not None:\n            # signal EOF to loop and wait\n            try:\n                self.queue.put_nowait(None)\n            except Exception:\n                pass\n            try:\n                await self._task\n            except asyncio.CancelledError:\n                pass\n            self._task = None\n        # flush remaining data\n        self._flush_batch(force=True)\n        logger.info(\"stop: recorder stopped and flushed\")\n\n    async def handle_frame(self, frame: AudioFrame) -> None:\n        self._handle_frame(frame)\n\n    def _handle_frame(self, frame: AudioFrame) -> None:\n        if self._samplerate is None:\n            self._samplerate = int(frame.sample_rate)\n        if self._channels is None:\n            try:\n                self._channels = len(frame.layout.channels)\n            except Exception:\n                arr = frame.to_ndarray()\n                self._channels = 1 if arr.ndim == 1 else int(arr.shape[0])\n\n        pcm = frame.to_ndarray()\n        # Ensure planar [C, S]\n        if pcm.ndim == 1:\n            pcm = pcm[np.newaxis, :]\n        # Convert to interleaved int16 mono for writing simplicity\n        if self._channels == 1:\n            data = pcm[0]\n        else:\n            # Downmix to mono for file size; adjust if stereo is needed\n            data = pcm.mean(axis=0).astype(pcm.dtype)\n        if data.dtype != np.int16:\n            if np.issubdtype(data.dtype, np.floating):\n                data = (np.clip(data, -1.0, 1.0) * 32767.0).astype(np.int16)\n            else:\n                data = data.astype(np.int16)\n\n        self._frames.append(data)\n        if len(self._frames) >= self.batch_frames:\n            self._flush_batch()\n\n    def _flush_batch(self, force: bool = False) -> None:\n        if not self._frames:\n            return\n        data = np.concatenate(self._frames, axis=0)\n        self._frames.clear()\n\n        sr = self._samplerate or 48000\n        ch = 1  # wrote mono above\n\n        filename = f\"{RECORDINGS_DIR}/batch_{self._idx:05d}.wav\"\n        self._idx += 1\n\n        with wave.open(filename, \"wb\") as wf:\n            wf.setnchannels(ch)\n            wf.setsampwidth(2)\n            wf.setframerate(sr)\n            wf.writeframes(data.tobytes())\n\n        logger.info(f\"saved {filename}\")\n"}
{"path": "./src_core/processors/base.py", "content": "import asyncio\nimport logging\nfrom typing import List, Optional\n\nfrom av import AudioFrame\n\nlogger = logging.getLogger(\"audio.BaseNode\")\n\n\nclass FanOutNode:\n    \"\"\"Common fan-out helpers for nodes that multiplex frames to multiple subscribers.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self) -> None:\n        self.out_queues: List[asyncio.Queue] = []\n\n    def subscribe(self) -> asyncio.Queue:\n        q: asyncio.Queue = asyncio.Queue()\n        self.out_queues.append(q)\n        return q\n\n    def unsubscribe(self, q: asyncio.Queue) -> None:\n        try:\n            self.out_queues.remove(q)\n        except ValueError:\n            pass\n\n    async def fan_out(self, frame: AudioFrame) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Enqueue a frame for all subscribers with drop-oldest backpressure.\"\"\"\n        for q in list(self.out_queues):\n            try:\n                q.put_nowait(frame)\n            except asyncio.QueueFull:\n                # drop-oldest until enqueue succeeds\n                while True:\n                    try:\n                        _ = q.get_nowait()\n                    except asyncio.QueueEmpty:\n                        break\n                    try:\n                        q.put_nowait(frame)\n                        break\n                    except asyncio.QueueFull:\n                        continue\n            except Exception as e:\n                logger.debug(f\"fan_out error: {e}\")\n\n    async def close_downstreams(self) -> None:\n        for q in list(self.out_queues):\n            try:\n                q.put_nowait(None)\n            except Exception:\n                pass\n            self.unsubscribe(q)\n\n\nclass ConsumerNode(FanOutNode):\n    \"\"\"\n    A base class for nodes that consume frames from an upstream source and optionally\n    emit frames to downstream subscribers.\n\n    Subclasses must implement async handle_frame(frame: AudioFrame) -> None\n    and call await self.fan_out(frame_or_new) when they want to forward.\n\n    Supports late binding: node may be constructed without a source frame = await self.queue.get()\n                if frame is None:\n                    break\n                await self.handle_frame(frame)\n        finally:\n            await {"path": "./src_core/processors/track_source_node.py", "content": "import asyncio\nfrom fractions import Fraction\nfrom typing import List\n\nimport av\nimport numpy as np\nfrom av import AudioFrame\n\n\nclass TrackSourceNode:\n    \"\"\"\n    Audio source node wrapping an aiortc MediaStreamTrack and normalizing audio:\n    - Fixed frame duration (frame_duration_ms)\n    - Fixed sample rate (target_rate)\n    - Fixed channel count (target_channels)\n    - Planar s16p numpy representation internally, emits AudioFrame in 's16p'\n\n    Responsibilities:\n    - Pull frames from aiortc track, resample/convert to the target format\n    - Slice into equal-length chunks, pad the tail if needed\n    - Monotonic PTS based on time_base = 1 / target_rate\n    - Fan-out frames to subscribers via asyncio.Queue\n\n    Does NOT handle losses/delay classification. That logic belongs to LossFillerNode.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, track,\n                 frame_duration_ms: int = 20,\n                 target_rate: int = 48000,\n                 target_output_format: str = 's16p',\n                 target_channels: int = 1):\n        \"\"\"Initialize source normalization.\n\n        Args:\n            track: aiortc MediaStreamTrack (audio) to read from.\n            frame_duration_ms: desired duration of emitted chunks (e.g., 20 ms).\n            target_rate: output sample rate (Hz), typically 48000 for WebRTC.\n            target_output_format: PyAV audio format string; default 's16p' (planar int16).\n            target_channels: output channel count (1=mono, 2=stereo).\n        \"\"\"\n        self.track = track\n        self.frame_duration_ms = frame_duration_ms\n        self.target_rate = target_rate\n        self.target_channels = target_channels\n\n        self.layout_name = \"mono\" if target_channels == 1 else \"stereo\"\n        self.output_format = target_output_format\n\n        self.resampler = av.audio.resampler.AudioResampler(\n            format=self.output_format,\n            layout=self.layout_name,\n            rate=self.target_rate,\n        )\n\n        self.queues: List[asyncio.Queue] = []\n        self.running = False\n\n        self._time_base = Fraction(1, self.target_rate)\n        self._samples_per_frame = int(self.target_rate * self.frame_duration_ms / 1000)\n        \n        # Pre-allocate padding buffer for performance\n        self._padding_buffer = np.zeros((self.target_channels, self._samples_per_frame), dtype=np.int16)\n\n    def samples_per_frame(self) -> int:\n        \"\"\"Return the fixed number of samples per emitted frame.\n\n        Calculated as int(target_rate * frame_duration_ms / 1000).\n        \"\"\"\n        return self._samples_per_frame\n\n    def subscribe(self) -> asyncio.Queue:\n        \"\"\"Subscribe to normalized frames stream.\n\n        Returns a new asyncio.Queue which will receive AudioFrame items and\n        a terminal None (EOF) when the source stops.\n        \"\"\"\n        q = asyncio.Queue()\n        self.queues.append(q)\n        return q\n\n    def unsubscribe(self, q: asyncio.Queue) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Unsubscribe a previously returned queue to allow GC of buffered frames.\"\"\"\n        try:\n            self.queues.remove(q)\n        except ValueError:\n            pass\n\n    async def start(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Run producer loop and fan-out normalized frames to subscribers.\n\n        Reads from aiortc track, normalizes to target format, and enqueues frames\n        to all subscriber queues. On cancellation or stop, enqueues None (EOF)\n        to each subscriber and clears internal references.\n        \"\"\"\n        if self.running:\n            return\n        self.running = True\n\n        try:\n            async for frame in self._normalized_chunk_stream():\n                for q in list(self.queues):\n                    try:\n                        q.put_nowait(frame)\n                    except asyncio.QueueFull:\n                        # drop-oldest policy until we can enqueue\n                        while True:\n                            try:\n                                _ = q.get_nowait()\n                            except asyncio.QueueEmpty:\n                                break\n                            try:\n                                q.put_nowait(frame)\n                                break\n                            except asyncio.QueueFull:\n                                continue\n        except asyncio.CancelledError:\n            raise\n        finally:\n            for q in list(self.queues):\n                q.put_nowait(None)\n                self.unsubscribe(q)\n            self.running = False\n\n    def _normalize_pcm_shape(self, pcm: np.ndarray, rf: AudioFrame) -> np.ndarray:\n        \"\"\"Normalize PCM array to (channels, samples) shape.\"\"\"\n        if pcm.ndim == 1:\n            return pcm[np.newaxis, :]\n        elif pcm.ndim == 2 and pcm.shape[0] == rf.samples and pcm.shape[1] == rf.layout.channels:\n            return pcm.T\n        return pcm\n\n    def _convert_channels(self, pcm: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:\n        \"\"\"Convert PCM to target channel count.\"\"\"\n        C = pcm.shape[0]\n        if C == self.target_channels:\n            return pcm\n        \n        if self.target_channels == 1:\n            # Downmix to mono\n            return pcm.mean(axis=0, keepdims=True).astype(pcm.dtype)\n        elif self.target_channels > C:\n            # Upmix by repeating last channel\n            return np.repeat(pcm[-1:, :], self.target_channels, axis=0)\n        else:\n            # Downmix by truncating channels\n            return pcm[:self.target_channels, :]\n\n    def _convert_to_int16(self, pcm: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:\n        \"\"\"Convert PCM to int16 format if needed.\"\"\"\n        if pcm.dtype == np.int16:\n            return pcm\n        return (np.clip(pcm, -1.0, 1.0) * 32767.0).astype(np.int16)\n\n    async def _normalized_chunk_stream(self):\n        \"\"\"Async generator yielding normalized AudioFrame chunks.\n\n        - Receives AudioFrame from aiortc\n        - Resamples/normalizes to 's16p' with target rate/layout\n        - Slices into equal-length frames, padding the tail of each chunk\n        - Sets PTS monotonically with step = samples_per_frame\n        \"\"\"\n        next_pts = 0\n        spf = self._samples_per_frame\n        target_channels = self.target_channels\n        output_format = self.output_format\n        layout_name = self.layout_name\n        target_rate = self.target_rate\n        time_base = self._time_base\n        \n        while True:\n            try:\n                in_frame: AudioFrame = await self.track.recv()\n            except Exception:\n                break\n\n            resampled = self.resampler.resample(in_frame)\n            frames = resampled if isinstance(resampled, list) else [resampled]\n\n            for rf in frames:\n                pcm = self._normalize_pcm_shape(rf.to_ndarray(), rf)\n                pcm = self._convert_channels(pcm)\n                pcm = self._convert_to_int16(pcm)\n\n                S = pcm.shape[1]\n                i = 0\n                while i < S:\n                    take = min(spf, S - i)\n                    if take == spf:\n                        # Fast path: no padding needed\n                        piece = pcm[:, i:i + spf]\n                    else:\n                        # Slow path: padding required\n                        piece = self._padding_buffer[:, :spf].copy()\n                        piece[:, :take] = pcm[:, i:i + take]\n\n                    out = AudioFrame.from_ndarray(piece, format=output_format, layout=layout_name)\n                    out.sample_rate = target_rate\n                    out.time_base = time_base\n                    out.pts = next_pts\n                    next_pts += spf\n                    yield out\n                    i += take\n\n"}
{"path": "./src_core/processors/echo_track_node.py", "content": "import asyncio\nfrom typing import Optional\n\nfrom aiortc import AudioStreamTrack\nfrom av import AudioFrame\nimport av\nimport logging\n\nlogger = logging.getLogger(\"audio.EchoTrackNode\")\n\n\nclass EchoTrackNode(AudioStreamTrack):\n    def __init_subclass__(cls, **kwargs):\n        return super().__init_subclass__(**kwargs)\n\n    \"\"\"\n    Audio sink/source node for WebRTC echo.\n\n    Subscribes to a source node (with subscribe() -> asyncio.Queue) and exposes\n    frames via AudioStreamTrack.recv() to be sent back over RTCPeerConnection.\n\n    Responsibilities: play back audio to the browser only. No transformation,\n    no modification of the stream, no influence on upstream nodes.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, source_node) -> None:\n        super().__init__()\n        self.source_node = source_node\n        self.queue: asyncio.Queue = source_node.subscribe()\n        self._closed = False\n        logger.info(\"initialized and subscribed to source\")\n\n    async def recv(self) -> AudioFrame:\n        if self._closed:\n            raise asyncio.CancelledError\n        # get first frame\n        frame = await self.queue.get()\n        if frame is None:\n            raise asyncio.CancelledError\n        # drain backlog aggressively to keep echo near real-time\n        try:\n            leave = 1\n            last = frame\n            while self.queue.qsize() > leave:\n                maybe = self.queue.get_nowait()\n                if maybe is None:\n                    break\n                last = maybe\n            frame = last\n        except Exception:\n            pass\n        # Frames are already normalized to s16p in TrackSourceNode; convert to packed on the fly if needed\n        try:\n            if frame.format.name == 's16p':\n                # repack to s16 without resampling\n                out = AudioFrame.from_ndarray(frame.to_ndarray(), format='s16', layout=frame.layout.name)\n                out.sample_rate = frame.sample_rate\n                out.pts = frame.pts\n                out.time_base = frame.time_base\n                return out\n        except Exception:\n            pass\n        return frame\n\n    def stop(self):\n        if self._closed:\n            return\n        self._closed = True\n        try:\n            self.queue.put_nowait(None)\n        except Exception:\n            pass\n        try:\n            self.source_node.unsubscribe(self.queue)\n        except Exception:\n            pass\n        logger.info(\"EchoTrackNode stopped and unsubscribed\")\n        try:\n            super().stop()\n        except Exception:\n            pass\n"}

{"path": "./src_core/processors/graph.py", "content": "import asyncio\nimport inspect\nimport logging\nfrom typing import List\n\nlogger = logging.getLogger(\"audio.Graph\")\n\n\nclass AudioGraph:\n    \"\"\"\n    Simple graph orchestrator for audio processing nodes.\n\n    Responsibilities:\n    - Keep a registry of nodes participating in the graph\n    - Start nodes: for async start() create tasks, for sync start() call directly\n    - Stop nodes: call stop() if provided and cancel background tasks\n\n    A node may implement:\n    - subscribe() -> asyncio.Queue            # to expose frames\n    - start() -> coroutine or None            # to start consuming/producing\n    - stop() -> coroutine or None             # to gracefully stop (optional)\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self) -> None:\n        self.nodes: List[object] = []\n        self.tasks: List[asyncio.Task] = []\n        self.started: bool = False\n\n    def add(self, node: object) -> object:\n        self.nodes.append(node)\n        return node\n\n    async def start(self) -> None:\n        if self.started:\n            return\n        self.started = True\n\n        for node in self.nodes:\n            start_fn = getattr(node, \"start\", None)\n            if start_fn is None:\n                continue\n            try:\n                ret = start_fn()\n                if inspect.isawaitable(ret):\n                    task = asyncio.create_task(ret)\n                    self.tasks.append(task)\n                    logger.info(f\"started async node {node.__class__.__name__}\")\n                else:\n                    logger.info(f\"started sync node {node.__class__.__name__}\")\n            except Exception as e:\n                logger.error(f\"node start error in {node.__class__.__name__}: {e}\")\n\n    async def stop(self) -> None:\n        # try graceful stop() on nodes first\n        for node in self.nodes:\n            stop_fn = getattr(node, \"stop\", None)\n            if stop_fn is None:\n                continue\n            try:\n                ret = stop_fn()\n                if inspect.isawaitable(ret):\n                    await ret\n                    logger.info(f\"stopped async node {node.__class__.__name__}\")\n                else:\n                    logger.info(f\"stopped sync node {node.__class__.__name__}\")\n            except Exception as e:\n                logger.error(f\"node stop error in {node.__class__.__name__}: {e}\")\n\n        # cancel any remaining tasks\n        for t in self.tasks:\n            if not t.done():\n                t.cancel()\n                try:\n                    await t\n                except asyncio.CancelledError:\n                    pass\n                except Exception as e:\n                    logger.error(f\"task cancel error: {e}\")\n        self.tasks.clear()\n        self.started = False\n"}

logging.getLogger(\"server.voice_commands.registry\")\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass VoiceCommand:\n    name: str\n    patterns: List[str]\n    handler: Callable[[Dict[str, Any]], Awaitable[Dict[str, Any]]]\n    description: str = \"\"\n    priority: int = 0\n\n\nclass CommandRegistry:\n    def __init__(self):\n        self.commands: Dict[str, VoiceCommand] = {}\n        self._compiled_patterns: Dict[str, List[re.Pattern]] = {}\n\n    def register(\n        self,\n        name: str,\n        patterns: List[str],\n        handler: Callable[[Dict[str, Any]], Awaitable[Dict[str, Any]]],\n        description: str = \"\",\n        priority: int = 0,\n    ) -> None:\n        command = VoiceCommand(\n            name=name,\n            patterns=patterns,\n            handler=handler,\n            description=description,\n            priority=priority,\n        )\n\n        self.commands[name] = command\n        self._compiled_patterns[name] = [re.compile(pattern, re.IGNORECASE) for pattern in normalized_text = text.strip().lower()\n        matches = []\n\n        for name, command in self.commands.items():\n            patterns = self._compiled_patterns[name]\n\n            for pattern in patterns:\n                match = pattern.search(normalized_text)\n                if match:\n                    params = match.groupdict()\n                    matches.append((command.priority, name, params))\n                    break\n\n        if not matches:\n            return None\n\n        matches.sort(reverse=True, key=lambda x: x[0])\n        _, command_name, params = matches[0]\n\n        return command_name, params\n\n    async def execute(self, command_name: str, params: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n        command = self.commands.get(command_name)\n        if not command:\n            logger.warning(\"Command not found: %s\", command_name)\n            return {\"error\": \"Command not found\"}\n\n        try:\n            result = await command.handler(params)\n            logger.info(\"Executed command: %s\", command_name)\n            return result\n        except Exception as exc:\n            logger.error(\"Error executing command %s: %s\", command_name, exc)\n            return {\"error\": str(exc)}\n"}
{"path": "./src_core/voice_commands/command_matcher.py", "content": "\"\"\"\nCommand Matcher - \u0441\u043e\u043f\u043e\u0441\u0442\u0430\u0432\u043b\u0435\u043d\u0438\u0435 \u0442\u0435\u043a\u0441\u0442\u0430 \u0441 \u0437\u0430\u0440\u0435\u0433\u0438\u0441\u0442\u0440\u0438\u0440\u043e\u0432\u0430\u043d\u043d\u044b\u043c\u0438 \u0433\u043e\u043b\u043e\u0441\u043e\u0432\u044b\u043c\u0438 \u043a\u043e\u043c\u0430\u043d\u0434\u0430\u043c\u0438.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport logging\nfrom typing import Dict, Any, Optional\nfrom .command_registry import CommandRegistry\n\nlogger = logging.getLogger(\"server.voice_commands.matcher\")\n\n\nclass CommandMatcher:\n    def __init__(self, registry: CommandRegistry):\n        self.registry = registry\n\n    async def process_transcription(self, text: str) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:\n        match = self.registry.match(text)\n\n        if not match:\n            logger.debug(\"No command matched for text: %s\", text)\n            return None\n\n        command_name, params = match\n        logger.info(\"Matched command: %s with params: %s\", command_name, params)\n\n        result = await self.registry.execute(command_name, params)\n\n        return {\n            \"command\": command_name,\n            \"params\": params,\n            \"result\": result,\n        }\n\n    def get_command_help(self) -> str:\n        commands = self.registry.get_all_commands()\n\n        if not commands:\n            return \"No commands registered\"\n\n        lines = [\"Available voice commands:\", \"\"]\n\n        for cmd in sorted(commands, key=lambda c: (-c.priority, c.name)):\n            lines.append(f\"\u2022 {cmd.name}\")\n            if cmd.description:\n                lines.append(f\"  {cmd.description}\")\n            lines.append(f\"  Patterns: {len(cmd.patterns)}\")\n            lines.append(\"\")\n\n        return \"\\n\".join(lines)\n"}
